1	"""QLoRA PEFT configuration and adapter management.
2	
3	All GPU library imports (peft, transformers, torch) are deferred inside
4	function bodies to ensure CPU-only importability (INFRA-05).
5	"""
6	
7	from __future__ import annotations
8	
9	from typing import Any
10	
11	
12	def build_qlora_config(
13	    rank: int,
14	    alpha: int,
15	    target_modules: list[str],
16	    dropout: float = 0.1,
17	) -> Any:
18	    """Build a QLoRA configuration for PEFT fine-tuning.
19	
20	    Args:
21	        rank: LoRA rank (dimensionality of low-rank matrices).
22	        alpha: LoRA alpha scaling factor.
23	        target_modules: List of module names to apply LoRA to.
24	        dropout: Dropout probability for LoRA layers.
25	
26	    Returns:
27	        A peft LoraConfig instance configured for QLoRA.
28	
29	    Example:
30	        >>> config = build_qlora_config(rank=64, alpha=128, target_modules=["q_proj"])
31	    """
32	    from peft import LoraConfig  # deferred — GPU/peft not available in CPU CI
33	
34	    return LoraConfig(
35	        r=rank,
36	        lora_alpha=alpha,
37	        target_modules=target_modules,
38	        lora_dropout=dropout,
39	        bias="none",
40	        task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
41	    )
42	
43	
44	def apply_lora_adapter(model: Any, config: Any) -> Any:
45	    """Apply a LoRA adapter to a base model.
46	
47	    Args:
48	        model: The base model to wrap with LoRA.
49	        config: The LoRA configuration (from build_qlora_config).
50	
51	    Returns:
52	        The model wrapped with a LoRA adapter via peft.get_peft_model.
53	
54	    Example:
55	        >>> adapted_model = apply_lora_adapter(base_model, lora_config)
56	    """
57	    from peft import get_peft_model  # deferred — GPU/peft not available in CPU CI
58	
59	    return get_peft_model(model, config)
60	
61	
62	def merge_adapter(model: Any) -> Any:
63	    """Merge LoRA weights into the base model.
64	
65	    Args:
66	        model: A PEFT model with LoRA adapter applied.
67	
68	    Returns:
69	        The base model with LoRA weights merged in.
70	
71	    Raises:
72	        NotImplementedError: Adapter merging is out of scope for Phase 21.
73	
74	    Example:
75	        >>> merged = merge_adapter(peft_model)
76	    """
77	    raise NotImplementedError(
78	        "merge_adapter is not yet implemented. "
79	        "It will merge LoRA weights into the base model and return the merged model."
80	    )
81

This plan closes all gaps while introducing a model registry for future model swaps.\n\n---\n\n## Step 1: Create Model Registry (`model_configs.py`)\n\n**New file:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/model_configs.py`\n\nNo GPU imports. or mc.default_lora_rank\n    alpha = alpha or mc.default_lora_alpha\n    grad_accum = gradient_accumulation_steps or mc.gradient_accumulation_steps\n    lr_sched = lr_scheduler_type or mc.lr_scheduler_type\n    attn_impl = mc.attn_implementation\n\nif attn_impl:\n    pass attn_implementation=attn_impl to AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained()\n\nif warm_start:\n    model = ──┬──> Step 3 (trainer.py)\n                             ├──> Step 4 (d2l_config.py)\nStep 2 (d2l_probe.py)  ─────┘\nStep 5 (exports) — after all above\n```\n\nSteps 1 and 2 are independent. Steps 3 and 4 depend on Step 1. Step 4 also depends on Step 2.\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\n1. **Unit tests (CPU CI):**\n   ```bash\n   cd libs/model-training && uv run pytest tests/ -v\n   ```\n   All existing + new tests pass. ## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Close Fine-Tuning Strategy Gaps

## Context

The LoRA fine-tuning strategy (`instructions/Report_2_LoRA_Fine_Tuning_Strategy.md`) defines a 2-stage pipeline: QLoRA bootstrapping with DeltaCoder warm-start on Qwen3.5-9B, then hypernetwork training. PR #19 delivered the data mining pipeline. The training code (`libs/model-training`) has 5 gaps blocking launch: no DeltaCoder warm-start, narrow target modules, no loss masking, hyperparameter mismatches, and hardcoded Qwen3-Coder-Next assumptions. This plan closes all gaps while introducing a model registry for future model swaps.

---

## Step 1: Create Model Registry (`model_configs.py`)

**New file:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/model_configs.py`

No GPU imports. Pure Pydantic.

**`ModelConfig`** (frozen BaseModel):
| Field | Type | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `canonical_name` | `str` | Registry lookup key |
| `model_id` | `str` | HuggingFace repo ID |
| `warm_start_adapter_id` | `str \| None` | Pre-trained adapter to continue from |
| `default_lora_rank` | `int` | LoRA rank |
| `default_lora_alpha` | `int` | LoRA alpha |
| `attn_implementation` | `str \| None` | e.g. `"eager"` (baked, not overridable per user decision) |
| `expected_num_layers` | `int` | Post-probe validation |
| `expected_hidden_size` | `int` | Post-probe validation |
| `gradient_accumulation_steps` | `int` | Training default |
| `lr_scheduler_type` | `str` | Training default |
| `epochs` | `int` | Training default |
| `quirks` | `dict[str, Any]` | Model-specific overrides |

**`ModelRegistry`** class:
- `register(config)`, `get(name) -> ModelConfig`, `list_names() -> list[str]`
- `default() -> ModelRegistry` classmethod returning singleton with built-in presets

**Built-in presets:**
- `"qwen3.5-9b"`: model_id=`"Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B"`, warm_start=`"danielcherubini/Qwen3.5-DeltaCoder-9B"`, rank=64, alpha=32, attn_impl=`"eager"`, layers=40, hidden=3584, grad_accum=16, lr_sched=`"constant"`, epochs=3
- `"qwen3-coder-next"`: model_id=`"Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next"`, warm_start=None, rank=8, alpha=16, attn_impl=None, layers=48, hidden=2048, grad_accum=4, lr_sched=`"cosine"`, epochs=3

**`validate_against_probe(config, probe_result)`**: warns on layer count or hidden size mismatch.

**New test file:** `tests/test_model_configs.py`
- Registry get/register/list, missing key raises, validation pass/mismatch, frozen config

---

## Step 2: Expand Probe Target Module Discovery (`d2l_probe.py`)

**File:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_probe.py`

**Changes:**
1. Add constants:
   ```python
   GDN_PROJECTIONS = {"in_proj_qkv", "in_proj_z", "in_proj_b", "in_proj_a", "out_proj"}
   MLP_PROJECTIONS = {"gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"}
   ALL_KNOWN_PROJECTIONS = ATTN_PROJECTIONS | GDN_PROJECTIONS | MLP_PROJECTIONS
   ```
2. Add `discover_target_modules(model) -> list[str]` — iterates `named_modules()`, collects Linear children matching any known projection, returns sorted deduplicated list
3. Extend `probe_model()` return dict to include `"target_modules"` key via `discover_target_modules()`
4. Export new symbols in `__all__`

**Test additions** in `tests/test_d2l_probe.py`:
- Fake models with GDN/MLP layers, verify discovery finds them
- Verify `probe_model()` result includes `"target_modules"` key

---

## Step 3: DeltaCoder Warm-Start + Hyperparameter Alignment (`trainer.py`)

**File:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py`

**Changes to `train_qlora()` signature** — add keyword-only params:
- `model_config_name: str | None = None` — registry lookup
- `warm_start_adapter_id: str | None = None` — explicit override
- `gradient_accumulation_steps: int | None = None` — defaults from registry
- `lr_scheduler_type: str | None = None` — defaults from registry

**Logic changes:**

```
if model_config_name:
    mc = ModelRegistry.default().get(model_config_name)
    model_id = mc.model_id (unless base_model_id explicitly set)
    warm_start = warm_start_adapter_id or mc.warm_start_adapter_id
    rank = rank or mc.default_lora_rank
    alpha = alpha or mc.default_lora_alpha
    grad_accum = gradient_accumulation_steps or mc.gradient_accumulation_steps
    lr_sched = lr_scheduler_type or mc.lr_scheduler_type
    attn_impl = mc.attn_implementation

if attn_impl:
    pass attn_implementation=attn_impl to AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained()

if warm_start:
    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, warm_start)
    # Enable gradient on adapter params
    for p in model.parameters():
        if hasattr(p, '_is_lora') or name contains 'lora_':
            p.requires_grad_(True)
    lora_config = None  # skip fresh LoRA
else:
    target_mods = probe cache target_modules or ["q_proj", "v_proj"]
    lora_config = build_qlora_config(rank, alpha, target_mods)
```

**SFTConfig changes:**
- `assistant_only_loss=True` (Gap 4 — loss masking)
- `gradient_accumulation_steps` from registry/param (was hardcoded 1)
- `lr_scheduler_type` from registry/param (was hardcoded "cosine")
- `peft_config=lora_config` (None when warm-starting)

**Update `train_and_register()`** to forward new params.

**Test additions** in `tests/test_trainer.py`:
- `test_warm_start_loads_peft_model` — mock PeftModel.from_pretrained, verify called
- `test_warm_start_skips_peft_config` — SFTTrainer gets peft_config=None
- `test_registry_config_sets_defaults` — rank/alpha/grad_accum/lr_sched from registry
- `test_assistant_only_loss_enabled` — SFTConfig has assistant_only_loss=True
- `test_fresh_init_backward_compat` — no model_config_name = existing behavior

---

## Step 4: D2L Config Model-Agnostic Refactor (`d2l_config.py`)

**File:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_config.py`

**Add** `build_hypernet_config(model_name: str, lora_r: int = 8, ...)`:
- Looks up `ModelRegistry.default().get(model_name)`
- Loads probe cache for model_name
- For Qwen3-Coder-Next: discovers layer_indices from `Qwen3NextConfig.layer_types` (existing logic)
- For other models (Qwen3.5-9B): uses probe cache `attention_layer_indices`
- Gets `target_modules` from probe cache (or fallback to `["q_proj", "v_proj"]`)
- Gets `feature_sizes` from probe cache (or fallback to hidden_size placeholder)
- Validates against `ModelConfig.expected_*` fields
- Returns `HypernetConfig`

**Refactor** `build_qwen3_hypernet_config()` to delegate: `return build_hypernet_config("qwen3-coder-next", ...)` — backward compat wrapper.

**File:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_train.py`

- Add `model_config_name: str = "qwen3.5-9b"` field to `D2LTrainConfig`
- Default `base_model_name` from registry: `ModelRegistry.default().get(model_config_name).model_id`
- Use `build_hypernet_config(config.model_config_name)` instead of `build_qwen3_hypernet_config()`

**Test updates:**
- `tests/test_d2l_config.py`: existing tests pass (wrapper delegates), add test for `build_hypernet_config("qwen3.5-9b")` with mocked probe cache

---

## Step 5: Export and Wire Up

**File:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/__init__.py`

Add `model_configs` to the public API exports (ModelConfig, ModelRegistry).

---

## Dependency Order

```
Step 1 (model_configs.py)  ──┬──> Step 3 (trainer.py)
                             ├──> Step 4 (d2l_config.py)
Step 2 (d2l_probe.py)  ─────┘
Step 5 (exports) — after all above
```

Steps 1 and 2 are independent. Steps 3 and 4 depend on Step 1. Step 4 also depends on Step 2.

   All existing + new tests pass. No GPU required.

4. **Backward compat:** `build_qwen3_hypernet_config()` still works, existing D2L tests pass.

```
1	"""Architecture probe and activation extraction for hypernetwork training.
2	
3	Discovers standard attention layers (those with q_proj/k_proj/v_proj/o_proj
4	children) via model.named_modules(), caches results to JSON, and provides
5	extract_activations_with_model() that accepts a pre-loaded model and tokenizer.
6	
7	Phase 26 purpose: eliminate hidden_size placeholders and per-call model loading.
8	The probe becomes the single source of truth for layer indices and projection
9	dimensions across the v7.0 pipeline.
10	
11	All heavy GPU imports (torch, transformers) are deferred to function bodies
12	per INFRA-05 project convention.
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import hashlib
18	import json
19	import logging
20	from datetime import datetime, timezone
21	from pathlib import Path
22	from typing import Any
23	
24	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
25	
26	ATTN_PROJECTIONS = {"q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"}
27	PROBE_CACHE_DIR = Path.home() / ".cache" / "rune" / "probes"
28	QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME = "qwen3-coder-next"
29	
30	__all__ = [
31	    "probe_model",
32	    "extract_activations_with_model",
33	    "load_probe_cache",
34	    "save_probe_cache",
35	    "QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME",
36	]
37	
38	
39	def _model_name_to_cache_path(model_name: str) -> Path:
40	    """Map model name to its JSON cache file path.
41	
42	    Uses SHA-256 truncated to 16 hex chars for the filename.
43	
44	    Args:
45	        model_name: Canonical model identifier string.
46	
47	    Returns:
48	        Path under PROBE_CACHE_DIR.
49	    """
50	    h = hashlib.sha256(model_name.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
51	    return PROBE_CACHE_DIR / f"{h}.json"
52	
53	
54	def probe_model(model: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
55	    """Probe a model's architecture to discover standard attention layers.
56	
57	    Iterates model.named_modules() to find layers that have all four attention
58	    projection children (q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj). DeltaNet and other
59	    linear-attention layers that lack these projections are skipped.
60	
61	    For each discovered attention layer, captures the in/out dimensions of
62	    q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, and o_proj weights.
63	
64	    Args:
65	        model: Any nn.Module (typically a transformer model).
66	
67	    Returns:
68	        Dict with keys:
69	            - attention_layer_indices: sorted list of int layer indices
70	            - feature_sizes: dict mapping projection name to {"in": int, "out": int}
71	    """
72	    attention_layer_indices: list[int] = []
73	    feature_sizes: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
74	
75	    for name, module in model.named_modules():
76	        # Get immediate children names
77	        child_names = {n.split(".")[-1] for n, _ in module.named_children()}
78	        if not ATTN_PROJECTIONS.issubset(child_names):
79	            continue
80	
81	        # Extract layer index from the last numeric segment in dotted name
82	        parts = name.split(".")
83	        layer_idx: int | None = None
84	        for part in reversed(parts):
85	            if part.isdigit():
86	                layer_idx = int(part)
87	                break
88	        if layer_idx is None:
89	            continue
90	
91	        attention_layer_indices.append(layer_idx)
92	
93	        # Capture projection dimensions (out_f, in_f = weight.shape)
94	        for proj_name in ("q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"):
95	            proj = getattr(module, proj_name, None)
96	            if proj is None or not hasattr(proj, "weight"):
97	                continue
98	            out_f, in_f = proj.weight.shape
99	            if proj_name not in feature_sizes:
100	                feature_sizes[proj_name] = {"in": in_f, "out": out_f}
101	
102	    return {
103	        "attention_layer_indices": sorted(attention_layer_indices),
104	        "feature_sizes": feature_sizes,
105	    }
106	
107	
108	def save_probe_cache(model_name: str, probe_result: dict[str, Any]) -> Path:
109	    """Persist probe results to JSON cache.
110	
111	    Adds metadata fields (model_name, model_name_hash, probed_at) to a copy
112	    of probe_result. Creates PROBE_CACHE_DIR if it does not exist.
113	
114	    Args:
115	        model_name: Canonical model identifier (used for cache lookup key).
116	        probe_result: Output from probe_model().
117	
118	    Returns:
119	        Path to the written JSON file.
120	    """
121	    cache_path = _model_name_to_cache_path(model_name)
122	    PROBE_CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
123	
124	    data = dict(probe_result)
125	    data["model_name"] = model_name
126	    data["model_name_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(model_name.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
127	    data["probed_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
128	
129	    cache_path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
130	    logger.info("Probe cache saved: %s → %s", model_name, cache_path)
131	    return cache_path
132	
133	
134	def load_probe_cache(model_name: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
135	    """Load probe results from JSON cache.
136	
137	    Args:
138	        model_name: Canonical model identifier.
139	
140	    Returns:
141	        Probe result dict (including metadata fields) if cached, else None.
142	        Never raises — returns None on any miss.
143	    """
144	    cache_path = _model_name_to_cache_path(model_name)
145	    if not cache_path.exists():
146	        logger.debug("Probe cache miss for '%s' (path: %s)", model_name, cache_path)
147	        return None
148	
149	    data: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(cache_path.read_text())
150	    logger.debug("Probe cache hit for '%s'", model_name)
151	    return data
152	
153	
154	def extract_activations_with_model(
155	    text: str,
156	    model: Any,
157	    tokenizer: Any,
158	    layer_indices: list[int] | None = None,
159	    model_name: str | None = None,
160	    max_length: int = 512,
161	) -> tuple[Any, Any]:
162	    """Extract per-layer hidden state activations from a pre-loaded model.
163	
164	    Runs text through the model with output_hidden_states=True and stacks
165	    activations from the specified layer indices. Uses hidden_states[i] directly
166	    (no +1 offset) — consistent with existing sakana_d2l.py convention.
167	
168	    Args:
169	        text: Input text to tokenize and process.
170	        model: Pre-loaded nn.Module in eval mode.
171	        tokenizer: Pre-loaded tokenizer.
172	        layer_indices: Which hidden state indices to extract. If None, loads
173	            from probe cache via model_name.
174	        model_name: Canonical model name for cache lookup (required when
175	            layer_indices is None).
176	        max_length: Max token sequence length.
177	
178	    Returns:
179	        Tuple of (features, attention_mask):
180	            features shape: (1, num_layers, seq_len, hidden_dim)
181	            attention_mask shape: (1, seq_len)
182	
183	    Raises:
184	        RuntimeError: If layer_indices is None and no probe cache exists for model_name.
185	    """
186	    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
187	
188	    if layer_indices is None:
189	        if model_name is None:
190	            msg = (
191	                "layer_indices is None and model_name is None — "
192	                "cannot load from probe cache without a model name."
193	            )
194	            raise RuntimeError(msg)
195	        cache = load_probe_cache(model_name)
196	        if cache is None:
197	            msg = (
198	                f"layer_indices is None but no probe cache found for '{model_name}'. "
199	                "Run probe_model() and save_probe_cache() first."
200	            )
201	            raise RuntimeError(msg)
202	        layer_indices = cache["attention_layer_indices"]
203	
204	    # Determine device from model
205	    try:
206	        device = next(model.parameters()).device
207	    except StopIteration:
208	        device = torch.device("cpu")
209	
210	    inputs = tokenizer(
211	        text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=True, max_length=max_length
212	    )
213	    inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
214	
215	    with torch.no_grad():
216	        outputs = model(**inputs, output_hidden_states=True)
217	
218	    hidden_states = outputs.hidden_states
219	    # Stack selected layers: (batch, num_layers, seq_len, hidden_dim)
220	    selected = torch.stack([hidden_states[i] for i in layer_indices], dim=1)
221	    attention_mask = inputs["attention_mask"]
222	
223	    logger.info(
224	        "Extracted activations: %s from %d layers", selected.shape, len(layer_indices)
225	    )
226	    return selected, attention_mask
227	
```

32	        super().__init__()

34	        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
35	        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)
36	        self.o_proj = nn.Linear(hidden * 2, hidden)

Args:\n            config: ModelConfig to register under its canonical_name.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._presets[config.canonical_name] = config\n\n    def get(self, name: str) -> ModelConfig:\n lr_scheduler_type=\"cosine\",\n                epochs=3,\n            )\n        )\n\n        cls._default_instance = registry\n        return registry\n\n\ndef validate_against_probe(\n    config: ModelConfig, probe_result: dict[str, Any]\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Validate a ModelConfig against probe results.\n\n    Checks expected_num_layers against discovered attention layer count and\n    f\"Probe found layer index {max_layer} but \"\n                f\"{config.canonical_name} expects {config.expected_num_layers} \"\n                f\"total layers\"\n            )\n            warnings.append(msg)\n            logger.warning(msg)\n\n    feature_sizes = probe_result.get(\"feature_sizes\", {})\n    for proj_name, dims in feature_sizes.items():\n        in_dim = dims.get(\"in\", 0)\n        if in_dim and in_dim != config.expected_hidden_size:\n            # Only warn for input projections (q/k/v) where in == hidden_size\n            if proj_name in (\"q_proj\", \"k_proj\", \"v_proj\"):\n                msg = (\n                    f\"{proj_name} input dim {in_dim} != expected \"\n                    f\"hidden_size {config.expected_hidden_size} for \"\n                    f\"{config.canonical_name}\"\n                )\n                warnings.append(msg)\n                logger.warning(msg)\n\n    if not warnings:\n        logger.info(\n            \"Probe validation passed for %s\", config.canonical_name\n        )\n\n    return warnings\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_probe.py",
  "old_string": "def probe_model(model: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Probe a model's architecture to discover standard attention layers.\n\n    Iterates model.named_modules() to find layers that have all four attention\n    projection children (q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj). DeltaNet and other\n    linear-attention layers that lack these projections are skipped.\n\n    For each discovered attention layer, captures the in/out dimensions of\n    q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, and o_proj weights.\n\n    Args:\n        model: Any nn.Module (typically a transformer model).\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with keys:\n            - attention_layer_indices: sorted list of int layer indices\n            - feature_sizes: dict mapping projection name to {\"in\": int, \"out\": int}\n    \"\"\"",
  "new_string": "def discover_target_modules(model: Any) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Discover all LoRA-targetable projection modules in a model.\n\n    Iterates model.named_modules() and collects the short names of any\n    Linear children whose names match known projection patterns (attention,\n    GDN, or MLP). Returns a sorted, deduplicated list.\n\n    Args:\n        model: Any nn.Module (typically a transformer model).\n\n    Returns:\n        Sorted list of unique projection names found (e.g.\n        [\"down_proj\", \"gate_proj\", \"k_proj\", \"o_proj\", \"q_proj\", ...]).\n    \"\"\"\n    import torch.nn as nn  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    found: set[str] = set()\n    for _name, module in model.named_modules():\n        for child_name, child in module.named_children():\n            short = child_name.split(\".\")[-1]\n            if short in ALL_KNOWN_PROJECTIONS and isinstance(child, nn.Linear):\n                found.add(short)\n    return sorted(found)\n\n\ndef probe_model(model: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Probe a model's architecture to discover attention layers and modules.\n\n    Iterates model.named_modules() to find layers that have all four attention\n    projection children (q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj). DeltaNet and other\n    linear-attention layers that lack these projections are skipped.\n\n    For each discovered attention layer, captures the in/out dimensions of\n    q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, and o_proj weights.\n\n    Also discovers all LoRA-targetable projection modules (attention, GDN,\n    MLP) via discover_target_modules().\n\n    Args:\n        model: Any nn.Module (typically a transformer model).\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with keys:\n            - attention_layer_indices: sorted list of int layer indices\n            - feature_sizes: dict mapping projection name to {\"in\": int, \"out\": int}\n            - target_modules: sorted list of discovered projection names\n    \"\"\""
}

  "new_string": "class _FakeGDNLayer(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Mimics a Qwen3.5 GDN layer with GDN projection children.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, hidden: int = 16) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n        self.in_proj_qkv = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden * 3)\n        self.in_proj_z = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)\n        self.in_proj_b = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)\n        self.in_proj_a = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)\n        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)\n\n\nclass _FakeMLPLayer(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Mimics a transformer MLP block with gate/up/down projections.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, hidden: int = 16) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n        self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden * 4)\n        self.up_proj = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden * 4)\n        self.down_proj = nn.Linear(hidden * 4, hidden)\n\n\nclass _FakeDeltaNetLayer(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Mimics a DeltaNet linear-attention layer — no q/k/v/o_proj.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, hidden: int = 16) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n        self.fc = nn.Linear(hidden, hidden)"

12	def _make_trajectory(tmp_path: Path, session_id: str, outcome: str) -> Path:
13	    """Write a trajectory JSON file to tmp_path and return its path."""
14	    traj = {
15	        "session_id": session_id,
16	        "task_description": "Write a hello world function",
17	        "task_type": "code-gen",
18	        "adapter_ids": [],
19	        "outcome": outcome,

22	            {
23	                "attempt": 1,
24	                "generated_code": "def hello(): return 'hello'",
25	                "tests_passed": True,
26	            }
27	        ],
28	    }
29	    traj_file = tmp_path / f"{session_id}.json"
30	    traj_file.write_text(json.dumps(traj))
31	    return traj_file

145	def _inject_fake_gpu_modules() -> None:
146	    """Inject minimal fake modules into sys.modules for CPU-only test runs."""
147	    fake_torch = ModuleType("torch")
148	    fake_torch.bfloat16 = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
149	
150	    fake_datasets = ModuleType("datasets")
151	
152	    def _fake_from_list(records: list) -> MagicMock:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
153	        return MagicMock()
154	
155	    fake_dataset_cls = MagicMock()
156	    fake_dataset_cls.from_list = staticmethod(_fake_from_list)
157	    fake_datasets.Dataset = fake_dataset_cls  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
158	
159	    fake_transformers = ModuleType("transformers")
160	    fake_transformers.AutoModelForCausalLM = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
161	    fake_transformers.AutoTokenizer = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
162	    fake_transformers.BitsAndBytesConfig = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
163	
164	    fake_trl = ModuleType("trl")
165	    fake_trl.SFTConfig = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
166	    fake_trl.SFTTrainer = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
167	
168	    fake_peft = ModuleType("peft")
169	    fake_peft.LoraConfig = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
170	    fake_peft.get_peft_model = MagicMock()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
171	
172	    sys.modules.setdefault("torch", fake_torch)
173	    sys.modules.setdefault("datasets", fake_datasets)
174	    sys.modules.setdefault("transformers", fake_transformers)
175	    sys.modules.setdefault("trl", fake_trl)
176	    sys.modules.setdefault("peft", fake_peft)
177	
178	
179	def _remove_fake_gpu_modules() -> None:
180	    """Remove injected fake modules (only if we injected them)."""
181	    for mod in ("torch", "datasets", "transformers", "trl", "peft"):
182	        if mod in sys.modules and isinstance(sys.modules[mod], ModuleType):
183	            # Only remove if it's actually a fake (not the real package)
184	            m = sys.modules[mod]
185	            if getattr(m, "__file__", "FAKE") is None:
186	                del sys.modules[mod]

Defaults to RUNE_BASE_MODEL env var\n            or \"Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\".\n        task_type: Task category (e.g. 'code-gen', 'bug-fix').\n        rank: LoRA rank.\n        alpha: LoRA alpha scaling factor.\n        epochs: Number of training epochs.\n        learning_rate: Optimizer learning rate.\n\n    Returns:\n        output_dir path where the adapter was saved.\n\n    Raises:\n        FileNotFoundError: If the trajectory file does not exist.\n        ValueError: If the trajectory is not successful or has no SFT messages.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Deferred GPU imports — all in function body for CPU-only importability\n    import torch\n    from datasets import Dataset\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig\n    from trl import SFTConfig, SFTTrainer\n\n    from model_training.peft_utils import build_qlora_config\n    from model_training.trajectory import format_for_sft, load_trajectory\n\n    # Load and format trajectory\n    trajectory = load_trajectory(session_id)  # raises FileNotFoundError if missing\n    messages = format_for_sft(trajectory)\n    if not messages:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Trajectory {session_id} is not successful or has no SFT messages\"\n        )\n\n    # Build dataset\n    dataset = Dataset.from_list([{\"messages\": messages}])\n\n    # NF4 quantization config (bfloat16 compute dtype prevents silent NaN loss)\n    bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(\n        load_in_4bit=True,\n        bnb_4bit_quant_type=\"nf4\",\n        bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,\n    )\n\n    # Load model and tokenizer\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        model_id,\n        quantization_config=bnb_config,\n        device_map=\"auto\",\n    )\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_id)\n\n    # Build LoRA config (bfloat16 is set in BitsAndBytesConfig, not LoraConfig)\n    lora_config = build_qlora_config(\n        rank=rank,\n        alpha=alpha,\n        target_modules=[\"q_proj\", \"v_proj\"],\n        dropout=0.1,\n    )\n\n    # Training arguments\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\n        output_dir=output_dir,\n        num_train_epochs=epochs,\n        learning_rate=learning_rate,\n        warmup_ratio=0.03,\n        lr_scheduler_type=\"cosine\",\n        bf16=True,\n        per_device_train_batch_size=1,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=1,\n        save_strategy=\"no\",\n        logging_steps=1,\n        report_to=\"none\",\n        eval_strategy=\"no\",\n    )\n\n    # Create and run trainer\n    trainer = SFTTrainer(\n        model=model,\n        args=training_args,\n        train_dataset=dataset,\n        peft_config=lora_config,\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\n    )\n    trainer.train()\n\n    # Save adapter weights only (PEFT-aware: safetensors + adapter_config.json)\n    trainer.save_model(output_dir)\n\n    return output_dir",
  "new_string": "def train_qlora(\n    session_id: str,\n    adapter_id: str,\n    output_dir: str,\n    *,\n    base_model_id: str | None = None,\n    task_type: str = \"code-gen\",\n    rank: int | None = None,\n    alpha: int | None = None,\n    epochs: int | None = None,\n    learning_rate: float = 2e-4,\n    model_config_name: str | None = None,\n    warm_start_adapter_id: str | None = None,\n    gradient_accumulation_steps: int | None = None,\n    lr_scheduler_type: str | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Train a QLoRA adapter from a recorded coding trajectory.\n\n    Orchestrates the full training pipeline: load trajectory, format as SFT\n    messages, build dataset, load model with NF4 quantization, train with SFT,\n    and save the adapter to output_dir.\n\n    When model_config_name is provided, training defaults (rank, alpha, LR\n    schedule, gradient accumulation, attention implementation) are loaded from\n    the model registry. When the registry config specifies a warm_start_adapter_id,\n    the pre-trained adapter is loaded via PeftModel.from_pretrained and training\n    continues from those weights instead of initializing fresh LoRA matrices.\n\n    All GPU imports are deferred to this function body; the module is safe to\n    import in CPU-only environments.\n\n    Args:\n        session_id: Trajectory session ID to train from.\n        adapter_id: Unique identifier for the resulting adapter.\n        output_dir: Directory to save the trained adapter weights.\n        base_model_id: HuggingFace model ID. Overrides registry default.\n        task_type: Task category (e.g. 'code-gen', 'bug-fix').\n        rank: LoRA rank. Defaults to registry value or 64.\n        alpha: LoRA alpha scaling factor. Defaults to registry value or 128.\n        epochs: Number of training epochs. Defaults to registry value or 3.\n        learning_rate: Optimizer learning rate.\n        model_config_name: Registry lookup key (e.g. \"qwen3.5-9b\"). When set,\n            populates defaults from the model registry.\n        warm_start_adapter_id: Pre-trained PEFT adapter to continue from.\n            Overrides the registry's warm_start_adapter_id.\n        gradient_accumulation_steps: Gradient accumulation steps. Defaults to\n            registry value or 16.\n        lr_scheduler_type: LR scheduler type. Defaults to registry value or\n            \"constant\".\n\n    Returns:\n        output_dir path where the adapter was saved.\n\n    Raises:\n        FileNotFoundError: If the trajectory file does not exist.\n        ValueError: If the trajectory is not successful or has no SFT messages.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Deferred GPU imports — all in function body for CPU-only importability\n    import torch\n    from datasets import Dataset\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig\n    from trl import SFTConfig, SFTTrainer\n\n    from model_training.peft_utils import build_qlora_config\n    from model_training.trajectory import format_for_sft, load_trajectory\n\n    # Resolve defaults from model registry if config name is provided\n    attn_impl: str | None = None\n    warm_start = warm_start_adapter_id\n    resolved_rank = rank if rank is not None else 64\n    resolved_alpha = alpha if alpha is not None else 128\n    resolved_epochs = epochs if epochs is not None else 3\n    resolved_grad_accum = gradient_accumulation_steps or 16\n    resolved_lr_sched = lr_scheduler_type or \"constant\"\n\n    if model_config_name:\n        from model_training.model_configs import ModelRegistry\n\n        mc = ModelRegistry.default().get(model_config_name)\n        if base_model_id is None:\n            base_model_id = mc.model_id\n        if warm_start is None:\n            warm_start = mc.warm_start_adapter_id\n        if rank is None:\n            resolved_rank = mc.default_lora_rank\n        if alpha is None:\n            resolved_alpha = mc.default_lora_alpha\n        if epochs is None:\n            resolved_epochs = mc.epochs\n        if gradient_accumulation_steps is None:\n            resolved_grad_accum = mc.gradient_accumulation_steps\n        if lr_scheduler_type is None:\n            resolved_lr_sched = mc.lr_scheduler_type\n        attn_impl = mc.attn_implementation\n\n    # Resolve model ID — read env var inside function body for monkeypatch testability\n    model_id = base_model_id or os.environ.get(\n        \"RUNE_BASE_MODEL\", \"Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct\"\n    )\n\n    # Load and format trajectory\n    trajectory = load_trajectory(session_id)  # raises FileNotFoundError if missing\n    messages = format_for_sft(trajectory)\n    if not messages:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Trajectory {session_id} is not successful or has no SFT messages\"\n        )\n\n    # Build dataset\n    dataset = Dataset.from_list([{\"messages\": messages}])\n\n    # NF4 quantization config (bfloat16 compute dtype prevents silent NaN loss)\n    bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(\n        load_in_4bit=True,\n        bnb_4bit_quant_type=\"nf4\",\n        bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,\n    )\n\n    # Load model and tokenizer\n    model_kwargs: dict[str, object] = {\n        \"quantization_config\": bnb_config,\n        \"device_map\": \"auto\",\n    }\n    if attn_impl:\n        model_kwargs[\"attn_implementation\"] = attn_impl\n\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_id, **model_kwargs)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_id)\n\n    # Warm-start: load pre-trained adapter and enable gradient on LoRA params\n    lora_config = None\n    if warm_start:\n        from peft import PeftModel\n\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, warm_start)\n        # Enable gradient on all adapter parameters for continued training\n        for name, param in model.named_parameters():\n            if \"lora_\" in name:\n                param.requires_grad_(True)\n    else:\n        # Fresh LoRA initialization — try probe cache for target modules\n        target_modules = [\"q_proj\", \"v_proj\"]\n        if model_config_name:\n            from model_training.d2l_probe import load_probe_cache\n\n            cache = load_probe_cache(model_config_name)\n            if cache and \"target_modules\" in cache:\n                target_modules = cache[\"target_modules\"]\n\n        lora_config = build_qlora_config(\n            rank=resolved_rank,\n            alpha=resolved_alpha,\n            target_modules=target_modules,\n            dropout=0.1,\n        )\n\n    # Training arguments\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\n        output_dir=output_dir,\n        num_train_epochs=resolved_epochs,\n        learning_rate=learning_rate,\n        warmup_ratio=0.03,\n        lr_scheduler_type=resolved_lr_sched,\n        bf16=True,\n        per_device_train_batch_size=1,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=resolved_grad_accum,\n        save_strategy=\"no\",\n        logging_steps=1,\n        report_to=\"none\",\n        eval_strategy=\"no\",\n        assistant_only_loss=True,\n    )\n\n    # Create and run trainer\n    trainer = SFTTrainer(\n        model=model,\n        args=training_args,\n        train_dataset=dataset,\n        peft_config=lora_config,\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\n    )\n    trainer.train()\n\n    # Save adapter weights only (PEFT-aware: safetensors + adapter_config.json)\n    trainer.save_model(output_dir)\n\n    return output_dir"
}

Defaults to RUNE_BASE_MODEL env var.\n        task_type: Task category (e.g. 'code-gen', 'bug-fix').\n        rank: LoRA rank.\n        alpha: LoRA alpha scaling factor.\n        epochs: Number of training epochs.\n        learning_rate: Optimizer learning rate.\n        database_url: SQLAlchemy database URL. Defaults to RUNE_DATABASE_URL env var\n            or \"sqlite:///{home}/.rune/rune.db\".\n\n    Returns:\n        adapter_id of the registered adapter.\n\n    Raises:\n        FileNotFoundError: If the trajectory file does not exist.\n        ValueError: If the trajectory is not successful or has no SFT messages.\n    \"\"\"",
  "new_string": "def train_and_register(\n    session_id: str,\n    adapter_id: str,\n    *,\n    base_model_id: str | None = None,\n    task_type: str = \"code-gen\",\n    rank: int | None = None,\n    alpha: int | None = None,\n    epochs: int | None = None,\n    learning_rate: float = 2e-4,\n    model_config_name: str | None = None,\n    warm_start_adapter_id: str | None = None,\n    gradient_accumulation_steps: int | None = None,\n    lr_scheduler_type: str | None = None,\n    database_url: str | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Train a QLoRA adapter and register it in the AdapterRegistry.\n\n    Combines train_qlora() with AdapterRegistry.store() to produce a fully\n    registered adapter ready for vLLM serving.\n\n    Args:\n        session_id: Trajectory session ID to train from.\n        adapter_id: Unique identifier for the resulting adapter.\n        base_model_id: HuggingFace model ID. Defaults to RUNE_BASE_MODEL env var.\n        task_type: Task category (e.g. 'code-gen', 'bug-fix').\n        rank: LoRA rank. Defaults to registry value or 64.\n        alpha: LoRA alpha scaling factor. Defaults to registry value or 128.\n        epochs: Number of training epochs. Defaults to registry value or 3.\n        learning_rate: Optimizer learning rate.\n        model_config_name: Registry lookup key (e.g. \"qwen3.5-9b\").\n        warm_start_adapter_id: Pre-trained PEFT adapter to continue from.\n        gradient_accumulation_steps: Gradient accumulation steps.\n        lr_scheduler_type: LR scheduler type.\n        database_url: SQLAlchemy database URL. Defaults to RUNE_DATABASE_URL env var\n            or \"sqlite:///{home}/.rune/rune.db\".\n\n    Returns:\n        adapter_id of the registered adapter.\n\n    Raises:\n        FileNotFoundError: If the trajectory file does not exist.\n        ValueError: If the trajectory is not successful or has no SFT messages.\n    \"\"\""
}

1	"""Config helpers for Qwen3-Coder-Next hypernetwork training.
2	
3	Requires transformers>=5.0 for Qwen3NextConfig (hybrid linear/full attention
4	architecture). transformers 5.3.0 is installed in this project.
5	
6	All heavy imports (transformers, ctx_to_lora, peft) are deferred to function
7	bodies per project convention (INFRA-05) to avoid GPU imports at module level.
8	"""
9	
10	from __future__ import annotations
11	
12	import logging
13	from typing import Any
14	
15	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
16	
17	__all__ = [
18	    "get_d2l_qwen3_config",
19	    "build_qwen3_hypernet_config",
20	    "build_hypernet_config",
21	]
22	
23	
24	def get_d2l_qwen3_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
25	    """Return Qwen3-Coder-Next architecture dimensions without loading model weights.
26	
27	    Uses Qwen3NextConfig defaults which exactly match Qwen3-Coder-Next specs:
28	    - hidden_size: 2048
29	    - num_hidden_layers: 48 (12 full_attention + 36 linear_attention)
30	    - num_attention_heads: 16 (Q heads), num_key_value_heads: 2 (GQA KV)
31	    - head_dim: 256
32	    - full_attention layer indices: [3, 7, 11, 15, 19, 23, 27, 31, 35, 39, 43, 47]
33	    - vocab_size: 151936
34	    - model_type: "qwen3_next"
35	
36	    Returns:
37	        Dict with keys: hidden_size, num_hidden_layers, num_attention_heads,
38	        num_key_value_heads, head_dim, attention_layer_indices, vocab_size,
39	        model_type.
40	    """
41	    from transformers import Qwen3NextConfig  # noqa: PLC0415
42	
43	    cfg = Qwen3NextConfig()
44	    layer_types: list[str] = cfg.layer_types or []
45	    attention_layer_indices = [
46	        i for i, t in enumerate(layer_types) if t == "full_attention"
47	    ]
48	    return {
49	        "hidden_size": cfg.hidden_size,
50	        "num_hidden_layers": cfg.num_hidden_layers,
51	        "num_attention_heads": cfg.num_attention_heads,
52	        "num_key_value_heads": cfg.num_key_value_heads,
53	        "head_dim": cfg.head_dim,
54	        "attention_layer_indices": attention_layer_indices,
55	        "vocab_size": cfg.vocab_size,
56	        "model_type": cfg.model_type,
57	    }
58	
59	
60	def build_qwen3_hypernet_config(
61	    lora_r: int = 8,
62	    target_modules: list[str] | None = None,
63	    aggregator_config: Any = None,
64	) -> Any:
65	    """Construct HypernetConfig targeting Qwen3-Coder-Next attention layers.
66	
67	    Discovers full_attention layer indices dynamically from Qwen3NextConfig.layer_types.
68	    Result has exactly 12 layer indices matching the Qwen3-Coder-Next architecture.
69	
70	    Phase 26 probe cache integration: if a probe cache exists for
71	    QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME, uses real per-projection in/out dimensions for
72	    feature_sizes. Falls back to hidden_size placeholder when no cache is found
73	    (e.g., in CI where the model has not been probed).
74	
75	    Args:
76	        lora_r: LoRA rank for the adapter. Defaults to 8.
77	        target_modules: LoRA target module names. Defaults to ["q_proj", "v_proj"].
78	        aggregator_config: Perceiver aggregator config from a Sakana checkpoint.
79	            If None (default / Phase 25 CI), HypernetConfig is built with
80	            aggregator_config=None as placeholder. Phase 29 populates this via
81	            get_aggregator_config() with a loaded model.
82	
83	    Returns:
84	        HypernetConfig with layer_indices set to the 12 full_attention indices
85	        and base_hidden_size=2048.
86	    """
87	    from ctx_to_lora.modeling.hypernet import HypernetConfig  # noqa: PLC0415
88	    from peft import LoraConfig  # noqa: PLC0415
89	    from transformers import Qwen3NextConfig  # noqa: PLC0415
90	
91	    if target_modules is None:
92	        target_modules = ["q_proj", "v_proj"]
93	
94	    cfg = Qwen3NextConfig()
95	    layer_types: list[str] = cfg.layer_types or []
96	    layer_indices = [
97	        i for i, t in enumerate(layer_types) if t == "full_attention"
98	    ]  # Always [3, 7, 11, 15, 19, 23, 27, 31, 35, 39, 43, 47]
99	
100	    lora_config = LoraConfig(
101	        r=lora_r,
102	        lora_alpha=lora_r * 2,
103	        target_modules=target_modules,
104	        lora_dropout=0.0,
105	        bias="none",
106	        task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
107	    )
108	
109	    from model_training.d2l_probe import (  # noqa: PLC0415
110	        QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME,
111	        load_probe_cache,
112	    )
113	
114	    cache = load_probe_cache(QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME)
115	    if cache is not None:
116	        in_sizes = {mod: cache["feature_sizes"][mod]["in"] for mod in target_modules}
117	        out_sizes = {mod: cache["feature_sizes"][mod]["out"] for mod in target_modules}
118	        feature_sizes: tuple[dict[str, int], dict[str, int]] = (in_sizes, out_sizes)
119	        logger.info("Using probe cache feature_sizes for %s", QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME)
120	    else:
121	        hidden: int = cfg.hidden_size or 2048
122	        _placeholder: dict[str, int] = dict.fromkeys(target_modules, hidden)
123	        feature_sizes = (_placeholder, dict.fromkeys(target_modules, hidden))
124	        logger.warning(
125	            "No probe cache for '%s' — using hidden_size=%d as placeholder. "
126	            "Run probe_model() and save_probe_cache() to set real dimensions.",
127	            QWEN3_NEXT_CANONICAL_NAME,
128	            cfg.hidden_size,
129	        )
130	
131	    return HypernetConfig(
132	        latent_size=512,
133	        use_light_weight_lora=False,
134	        light_weight_latent_size=128,
135	        per_rank_gen=False,
136	        use_per_rank_bias=False,
137	        use_bias=True,
138	        per_layer_processing=False,
139	        use_token_mixing=False,
140	        num_pre_head_layers=1,
141	        dropout_rate=0.0,
142	        lora_config=lora_config,
143	        extra_modules=None,
144	        base_hidden_size=cfg.hidden_size,
145	        layer_indices=layer_indices,
146	        feature_sizes=feature_sizes,
147	        aggregator_config=aggregator_config,
148	    )
149	
```

  "old_string": "    return HypernetConfig(\n        latent_size=512,\n        use_light_weight_lora=False,\n        light_weight_latent_size=128,\n        per_rank_gen=False,\n        use_per_rank_bias=False,\n        use_bias=True,\n        per_layer_processing=False,\n        use_token_mixing=False,\n        num_pre_head_layers=1,\n        dropout_rate=0.0,\n        lora_config=lora_config,\n        extra_modules=None,\n        base_hidden_size=cfg.hidden_size,\n        layer_indices=layer_indices,\n        feature_sizes=feature_sizes,\n        aggregator_config=aggregator_config,\n    )",
  "new_string": "    return HypernetConfig(\n        latent_size=512,\n        use_light_weight_lora=False,\n        light_weight_latent_size=128,\n        per_rank_gen=False,\n        use_per_rank_bias=False,\n        use_bias=True,\n        per_layer_processing=False,\n        use_token_mixing=False,\n        num_pre_head_layers=1,\n        dropout_rate=0.0,\n        lora_config=lora_config,\n        extra_modules=None,\n        base_hidden_size=cfg.hidden_size,\n        layer_indices=layer_indices,\n        feature_sizes=feature_sizes,\n        aggregator_config=aggregator_config,\n    )\n\n\ndef build_hypernet_config(\n    model_name: str,\n    lora_r: int | None = None,\n    target_modules: list[str] | None = None,\n    aggregator_config: Any = None,\n) -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Construct HypernetConfig for any registered model.\n\n    Uses the model registry for architecture expectations and the probe cache\n    for actual layer indices and feature dimensions. For Qwen3-Coder-Next,\n    delegates to the specialized builder. For other models, builds config\n    from probe cache data.\n\n    Args:\n        model_name: Canonical model name from the registry (e.g. \"qwen3.5-9b\").\n        lora_r: LoRA rank. Defaults to the registry's default_lora_rank.\n        target_modules: LoRA target module names. Defaults to probe cache's\n            target_modules or [\"q_proj\", \"v_proj\"].\n        aggregator_config: Perceiver aggregator config from a Sakana checkpoint.\n\n    Returns:\n        HypernetConfig configured for the specified model.\n\n    Raises:\n        KeyError: If model_name is not in the registry.\n        RuntimeError: If no probe cache exists for a non-Qwen3-Coder-Next model.\n    \"\"\"\n    from ctx_to_lora.modeling.hypernet import HypernetConfig  # noqa: PLC0415\n    from peft import LoraConfig  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    from model_training.d2l_probe import load_probe_cache  # noqa: PLC0415\n    from model_training.model_configs import (  # noqa: PLC0415\n        ModelRegistry,\n        validate_against_probe,\n    )\n\n    mc = ModelRegistry.default().get(model_name)\n\n    # Qwen3-Coder-Next has specialized logic for hybrid attention discovery\n    if model_name == \"qwen3-coder-next\":\n        rank = lora_r if lora_r is not None else mc.default_lora_rank\n        return build_qwen3_hypernet_config(\n            lora_r=rank,\n            target_modules=target_modules,\n            aggregator_config=aggregator_config,\n        )\n\n    # Generic path: requires probe cache for layer indices and dimensions\n    cache = load_probe_cache(model_name)\n    if cache is None:\n        msg = (\n            f\"No probe cache found for {model_name!r}. \"\n            \"Run probe_model() and save_probe_cache() before building \"\n            \"hypernet config for this model.\"\n        )\n        raise RuntimeError(msg)\n\n    validate_against_probe(mc, cache)\n\n    rank = lora_r if lora_r is not None else mc.default_lora_rank\n    layer_indices = cache[\"attention_layer_indices\"]\n\n    # Resolve target modules from probe cache or fallback\n    if target_modules is None:\n        target_modules = cache.get(\"target_modules\", [\"q_proj\", \"v_proj\"])\n\n    lora_config = LoraConfig(\n        r=rank,\n        lora_alpha=rank * 2,\n        target_modules=target_modules,\n        lora_dropout=0.0,\n        bias=\"none\",\n        task_type=\"CAUSAL_LM\",\n    )\n\n    # Build feature_sizes from probe cache\n    feature_sizes_raw = cache.get(\"feature_sizes\", {})\n    in_sizes: dict[str, int] = {}\n    out_sizes: dict[str, int] = {}\n    for mod in target_modules:\n        if mod in feature_sizes_raw:\n            in_sizes[mod] = feature_sizes_raw[mod][\"in\"]\n            out_sizes[mod] = feature_sizes_raw[mod][\"out\"]\n        else:\n            in_sizes[mod] = mc.expected_hidden_size\n            out_sizes[mod] = mc.expected_hidden_size\n\n    feature_sizes: tuple[dict[str, int], dict[str, int]] = (\n        in_sizes,\n        out_sizes,\n    )\n\n    return HypernetConfig(\n        latent_size=512,\n        use_light_weight_lora=False,\n        light_weight_latent_size=128,\n        per_rank_gen=False,\n        use_per_rank_bias=False,\n        use_bias=True,\n        per_layer_processing=False,\n        use_token_mixing=False,\n        num_pre_head_layers=1,\n        dropout_rate=0.0,\n        lora_config=lora_config,\n        extra_modules=None,\n        base_hidden_size=mc.expected_hidden_size,\n        layer_indices=layer_indices,\n        feature_sizes=feature_sizes,\n        aggregator_config=aggregator_config,\n    )"
}

63	class D2LTrainConfig(BaseModel):
64	    """Pydantic model for D2L training hyperparameters.
65	
66	    Enables validation, JSON serialization (for checkpoint storage), and
67	    `.model_dump()` for MLflow experiment logging.
68	
69	    Attributes:
70	        base_model_name: HuggingFace model name for the student/teacher base.
71	        sakana_checkpoint_path: Path to the Sakana hypernet checkpoint.
72	        num_steps: Total training steps.
73	        lr: Learning rate for AdamW optimizer.
74	        alpha: Blending weight for KL vs CE loss (1.0 = pure KL, 0.0 = pure CE).
75	        temperature: Softmax temperature for KL divergence computation.
76	        checkpoint_every: Steps between lightweight checkpoint saves.
77	        full_checkpoint_every: Steps between full checkpoint saves (incl. optimizer).
78	        checkpoint_dir: Directory for checkpoint output.
79	        experiment_name: MLflow experiment name.
80	        dry_run: If True, validate tensor shapes then exit.
81	        smoke_test: If True, run 5 steps and verify loss trend.
82	        dataset_path: Path to training JSONL file (required for full training).
83	        grad_clip: Gradient clipping max norm.
84	        warmup_steps: Number of linear LR warmup steps.
85	        lora_r: LoRA rank.
86	        max_length: Maximum tokenizer sequence length.
87	    """
88	
89	    base_model_name: str = Field(default="Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next")
90	    sakana_checkpoint_path: str
91	    num_steps: int = Field(default=100)
92	    lr: float = Field(default=2e-4)
93	    alpha: float = Field(default=0.5)
94	    temperature: float = Field(default=2.0)
95	    checkpoint_every: int = Field(default=100)
96	    full_checkpoint_every: int = Field(default=500)
97	    checkpoint_dir: str = Field(default="./checkpoints")
98	    experiment_name: str = Field(default="d2l-qwen3")

100	    smoke_test: bool = Field(default=False)
101	    dataset_path: str | None = Field(default=None)
102	    grad_clip: float = Field(default=1.0)
103	    warmup_steps: int = Field(default=10)

105	    max_length: int = Field(default=512)
106

112	            raise ValueError(f"lr must be > 0, got {v}")
113	        return v

120	            raise ValueError(f"alpha must be in [0, 1], got {v}")
121	        return v

129	        return v

  "old_string": "def test_build_qwen3_hypernet_config_base_hidden_size() -> None:\n    \"\"\"build_qwen3_hypernet_config sets base_hidden_size to 2048.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_config import build_qwen3_hypernet_config\n\n    hypernet_cfg = build_qwen3_hypernet_config()\n    assert hypernet_cfg.base_hidden_size == 2048",
  "new_string": "def test_build_qwen3_hypernet_config_base_hidden_size() -> None:\n    \"\"\"build_qwen3_hypernet_config sets base_hidden_size to 2048.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_config import build_qwen3_hypernet_config\n\n    hypernet_cfg = build_qwen3_hypernet_config()\n    assert hypernet_cfg.base_hidden_size == 2048\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# build_hypernet_config tests (model-agnostic)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_build_hypernet_config_delegates_qwen3_coder_next() -> None:\n    \"\"\"build_hypernet_config(\"qwen3-coder-next\") produces same result as direct.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_config import (\n        build_hypernet_config,\n        build_qwen3_hypernet_config,\n    )\n\n    direct = build_qwen3_hypernet_config()\n    via_registry = build_hypernet_config(\"qwen3-coder-next\")\n    assert list(via_registry.layer_indices) == list(direct.layer_indices)\n    assert via_registry.base_hidden_size == direct.base_hidden_size\n\n\ndef test_build_hypernet_config_qwen35_with_probe_cache(\n    tmp_path: object, monkeypatch: object,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"build_hypernet_config(\"qwen3.5-9b\") builds config from probe cache.\"\"\"\n    import model_training.d2l_probe as probe_module\n    from model_training.d2l_probe import save_probe_cache\n\n    # Use tmp_path for probe cache\n    monkeypatch.setattr(probe_module, \"PROBE_CACHE_DIR\", tmp_path)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n    # Populate a fake probe cache for qwen3.5-9b\n    probe_data = {\n        \"attention_layer_indices\": list(range(40)),\n        \"feature_sizes\": {\n            \"q_proj\": {\"in\": 3584, \"out\": 3584},\n            \"v_proj\": {\"in\": 3584, \"out\": 512},\n        },\n        \"target_modules\": [\"q_proj\", \"k_proj\", \"v_proj\", \"o_proj\"],\n    }\n    save_probe_cache(\"qwen3.5-9b\", probe_data)\n\n    from model_training.d2l_config import build_hypernet_config\n\n    hc = build_hypernet_config(\"qwen3.5-9b\")\n    assert list(hc.layer_indices) == list(range(40))\n    assert hc.base_hidden_size == 3584\n\n\ndef test_build_hypernet_config_unknown_model_raises() -> None:\n    \"\"\"build_hypernet_config raises KeyError for unregistered model.\"\"\"\n    import pytest\n\n    from model_training.d2l_config import build_hypernet_config\n\n    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match=\"nonexistent-model\"):\n        build_hypernet_config(\"nonexistent-model\")\n\n\ndef test_build_hypernet_config_missing_probe_raises(\n    tmp_path: object, monkeypatch: object,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"build_hypernet_config raises RuntimeError when no probe cache exists.\"\"\"\n    import pytest\n\n    import model_training.d2l_probe as probe_module\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(probe_module, \"PROBE_CACHE_DIR\", tmp_path)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n    from model_training.d2l_config import build_hypernet_config\n\n    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=\"probe cache\"):\n        build_hypernet_config(\"qwen3.5-9b\")"
}

All pass standalone. This is a pre-existing issue — when other test files load real `torch` (e.g., `test_d2l_probe.py` imports `torch`), the `setdefault` in `_inject_fake_gpu_modules` doesn't override the real module, then `bitsandbytes` fails because it's not installed.

This is a pre-existing test isolation issue, not something I introduced. Let me verify by checking if the old trainer tests also fail when run with the full suite.

                ("lora_A.weight", MagicMock()),
                ("lora_B.weight", MagicMock()),
            ]
>           fake_peft.PeftModel.from_pretrained.return_value = fake_wrapped
            ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E           AttributeError: 'method' object has no attribute 'return_value'

libs/model-training/tests/test_trainer.py:215: AttributeError
```

> AGENT

The issue is that when real `peft` is imported (from the d2l_config tests which import real torch/peft), `sys.modules.setdefault("peft", fake_peft)` doesn't override it. The real `PeftModel.from_pretrained` is a classmethod, not a MagicMock. 

The fix is to use `sys.modules["peft"] = fake_peft` (force override) instead of `setdefault`, but only for the tests that need deep mocking. Let me use `monkeypatch` instead.

> TOOL

> AGENT

All 5 steps complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary

**New files:**
- `model_configs.py` — `ModelConfig` (frozen Pydantic), `ModelRegistry` with presets for `qwen3.5-9b` (DeltaCoder warm-start, eager attention, rank-64) and `qwen3-coder-next`, plus `validate_against_probe()`
- `tests/test_model_configs.py` — 11 tests covering registry ops, frozen config, training defaults, probe validation

**Modified files:**

| File | Changes |
|---|---|
| `d2l_probe.py` | Added GDN/MLP projection constants, `discover_target_modules()`, extended `probe_model()` to return `target_modules` |
| `trainer.py` | DeltaCoder warm-start via `PeftModel.from_pretrained`, `model_config_name` param, `assistant_only_loss=True`, registry-driven defaults (grad_accum=16, lr_sched="constant", eager attention) |
| `d2l_config.py` | Added model-agnostic `build_hypernet_config(model_name)` that routes through registry + probe cache; refactored existing function description |
| `d2l_train.py` | Added `model_config_name` field to `D2LTrainConfig` (default "qwen3.5-9b"), `base_model_name` auto-resolves from registry, training loop uses `build_hypernet_config()` |
| `__init__.py` | Exports `ModelConfig`, `ModelRegistry` |
| `test_d2l_probe.py` | 6 new tests for GDN/MLP/full module discovery |
| `test_trainer.py` | 4 new tests + `_force_inject_fake_gpu_modules` for reliable test isolation |
| `test_d2l_config.py` | 4 new tests for model-agnostic config builder |

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"feat: model registry, DeltaCoder warm-start, training strategy alignment\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nCloses the gaps between the [LoRA Fine-Tuning Strategy](instructions/Report_2_LoRA_Fine_Tuning_Strategy.md) and the `model-training` implementation to prepare for Qwen3.5-9B fine-tuning launch.\n\n- **Model Registry** (`model_configs.py`): `ModelConfig` frozen dataclass + `ModelRegistry` with named presets for `qwen3.5-9b` (DeltaCoder warm-start, eager attention, rank-64, grad_accum=16, constant LR) and `qwen3-coder-next`. Factory pattern for easy future model swaps.\n- **DeltaCoder Warm-Start**: `train_qlora()` now accepts `model_config_name` and `warm_start_adapter_id`. When warm-starting, loads pre-trained adapter via `PeftModel.from_pretrained()` and continues training on existing LoRA weights.\n- **Assistant-Only Loss Masking**: `assistant_only_loss=True` in SFTConfig — computes loss only on assistant tokens, not system/user turns.\n- **Hyperparameter Alignment**: `gradient_accumulation_steps=16`, `lr_scheduler_type=\"constant\"`, `attn_implementation=\"eager\"` — all from the strategy document.\n- **Dynamic Target Module Discovery**: Probe now discovers GDN (`in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, etc.) and MLP (`gate_proj`, `up_proj`, `down_proj`) projections alongside attention modules.\n- **Model-Agnostic D2L Config**: New `build_hypernet_config(model_name)` uses registry + probe cache for any registered model. `D2LTrainConfig` defaults to `qwen3.5-9b`.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] 175 tests pass (`uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/ -v`)\n- [x] `ruff check` passes\n- [x] `mypy` passes (0 new errors; 3 pre-existing `import-not-found`)\n- [ ] GPU integration: probe Qwen3.5-9B, validate DeltaCoder loads via `PeftModel.from_pretrained`\n- [ ] Run HumanEval baseline with `scripts/eval/generate_completions.py`\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create pull request"
}
```

## PR #23 Summary

**Title**: feat: model registry, DeltaCoder warm-start, training strategy alignment

**Purpose**: Closes the gap between the LoRA Fine-Tuning Strategy document and the `model-training` implementation to prepare for Qwen3.5-9B fine-tuning launch.

### Key Changes

**New Files:**
- **`model_configs.py`** (200 lines) — Introduces `ModelConfig` (frozen Pydantic dataclass) and `ModelRegistry` with presets for `qwen3.5-9b` (DeltaCoder warm-start, eager attention, rank-64) and `qwen3-coder-next`. Factory pattern for model configuration.

**Modified Files:**

1. **`d2l_config.py`** — Adds `build_hypernet_config(model_name)` function. Model-agnostic builder that uses the registry + probe cache to support any registered model, not just Qwen3-Coder-Next.

2. **`d2l_probe.py`** — New `discover_target_modules()` function discovers GDN (`in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`) and MLP (`gate_proj`, `up_proj`, `down_proj`) projections alongside attention modules. Probe output now includes a `target_modules` field.

3. **`d2l_train.py`** — Refactors to use `model_config_name` instead of hard-coded `base_model_name`. Adds `model_post_init()` to resolve base model ID from registry. Updated calls to use `build_hypernet_config()`.

4. **`trainer.py`** — Major refactor:
   - Adds `_resolve_training_params()` helper to merge registry defaults with explicit overrides
   - `train_qlora()` now accepts `model_config_name`, `warm_start_adapter_id`, `gradient_accumulation_steps`, `lr_scheduler_type`
   - When warm-starting, loads pre-trained adapter via `PeftModel.from_pretrained()` and continues training
   - Supports `attn_implementation="eager"` parameter
   - `train_qlora_and_register()` passes new parameters through

5. **Tests** — Comprehensive test coverage added:
   - `test_d2l_config.py` (72 new lines) — Tests for model-agnostic config building
   - `test_d2l_probe.py` (141 new lines) — Tests for GDN/MLP projection discovery
   - `test_model_configs.py` (178 new lines) — Registry and model config tests
   - `test_trainer.py` (189 new lines, +1 existing) — Training parameter resolution and warm-start tests

**Hyperparameter Alignment** (from strategy document):
- `gradient_accumulation_steps=16`
- `lr_scheduler_type="constant"`
- `attn_implementation="eager"`

All tests pass (175 tests), linting clean, mypy clean (0 new errors).

Here are the significant bugs found in PR #23:

---

**1. `_resolve_training_params` silently ignores the `alpha` override when a registry config exists**

File: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py`

The diff shows `_resolve_training_params` is added, but the truncated diff doesn't show the full body. However, looking at the call site for `train_and_register`, there's a separate model_id resolution path that does NOT go through `_resolve_training_params` — it manually re-derives `model_id` from the registry instead of reusing `params`. The function `train_qlora` is then called with `rank=rank` (the raw, possibly-None argument), but `rank` was never defaulted at the call site:

```python
# In train_and_register:
        rank=rank if rank is not None else 64,
```

This correctly defaults `rank` to 64 for the `register_adapter` call, but `train_qlora` is called with the raw `rank` (possibly `None`), which will either fail or be resolved inside `train_qlora` via `_resolve_training_params`. This is inconsistent but likely harmless since `train_qlora` also calls `_resolve_training_params`. However, if `model_config_name` is provided to `train_and_register`, the registry-derived rank will be used in `train_qlora` but the registration call uses `rank if rank is not None else 64` — ignoring the registry's default rank. The registered adapter will record rank=64 even if the registry specifies a different default, causing a metadata mismatch.

---

**2. `discover_target_modules` double-counts via redundant nested iteration**

File: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_probe.py`

```python
for _name, module in model.named_modules():
    for child_name, child in module.named_children():
        short = child_name.split(".")[-1]
        if short in ALL_KNOWN_PROJECTIONS and isinstance(child, nn.Linear):
            found.add(short)
```

`model.named_modules()` already iterates every submodule recursively (including all intermediate containers). Then for each of those, `named_children()` is called, which walks direct children. This means every non-leaf module's children are visited once per ancestor that contains them. For a deep model with many layers, each projection module will be "found" many times — but since `found` is a set this doesn't cause incorrect results. The real bug is more subtle: `child_name.split(".")[-1]` on a `named_children()` result will always just be the plain child name (no dots), so the split is a no-op. The actual issue is that this double-iteration visits every Linear layer an exponential number of times relative to model depth, which can be extremely slow on large models with hundreds of layers. This should instead be a single `named_modules()` pass checking `isinstance(module, nn.Linear)` and using the last component of `_name`.

---

**3. `model_post_init` on a frozen Pydantic model has no guard against `KeyError`**

File: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_train.py`

```python
def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
    if not self.base_model_name:
        from model_training.model_configs import ModelRegistry
        mc = ModelRegistry.default().get(self.model_config_name)
        object.__setattr__(self, "base_model_name", mc.model_id)
```

`ModelRegistry.default().get(self.model_config_name)` will raise `KeyError` if `model_config_name` is not in the registry (e.g. a typo or an unregistered model name). The default value `"qwen3.5-9b"` works, but any user-provided `--model-config-name` that doesn't match a registry entry will produce an unhandled `KeyError` inside `model_post_init`, which surfaces as a cryptic Pydantic validation error rather than a clear "model config not found" message. This is a missing error boundary at a system boundary (user input -> registry lookup).

---

**4. `build_hypernet_config` raises `RuntimeError` for non-Qwen3-Coder-Next models with no probe cache, but the error message may reference the wrong variable**

File: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_config.py`

The diff shows the new `build_hypernet_config` raises `RuntimeError` when no probe cache exists for non-Qwen3 models. Given the truncated diff, this is a potential issue rather than a confirmed bug — if `_require_probe_cache` is called with `config.model_config_name` in `d2l_train.py` before `build_hypernet_config`, the error will surface at probe-check time (good). But `build_hypernet_config` itself is exported and callable directly, and callers who skip the probe-check step will get a `RuntimeError` from inside `build_hypernet_config` with no prior indication of what's needed.

---

**Summary of significant bugs:**

| # | Severity | File | Issue |
|---|----------|------|-------|
| 1 | Medium | `trainer.py` | `train_and_register` registers `rank=64` hardcoded instead of using the registry-resolved rank, causing metadata mismatch when `model_config_name` provides a different default |
| 2 | Medium | `d2l_probe.py` | `discover_target_modules` is O(depth * modules) instead of O(modules) due to nested `named_modules` + `named_children` iteration — will be very slow on real models |
| 3 | Low-Medium | `d2l_train.py` | Unhandled `KeyError` in `model_post_init` when `model_config_name` is not in registry, producing opaque Pydantic errors |

I've now read the complete diff. Let me analyze everything against CLAUDE.md's conventions.

Here is my audit of PR #23 against CLAUDE.md conventions.

---

## Issues Found

### 1. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/__init__.py` — Top-level GPU-adjacent import

The new line `from model_training.model_configs import ModelConfig, ModelRegistry` is added at module level in `__init__.py`. `model_configs.py` itself is safe (no GPU imports), but the pattern matters: `__init__.py` is the public surface of `model_training`, and this file already imports `from model_training.d2l_train import D2LTrainConfig, train_d2l_qwen3` at module level — `d2l_train` is a GPU-heavy module. The new import is not itself a violation (no GPU deps in `model_configs.py`), but the PR description claims "No GPU imports — safe to import anywhere." The underlying risk is that `model_training.__init__` is not CPU-safe regardless, so adding to its top-level imports is not a new INFRA-05 violation specifically introduced by this PR. This is a pre-existing condition, not a new violation. **No flag.**

### 2. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py` — GPU import inside conditional block, not inside function body (INFRA-05 violation)

In `train_qlora()`, the warm-start path does:

```python
if warm_start:
    from peft import PeftModel
```

This import is deferred inside a function body, which is correct per INFRA-05. **No issue here.**

However, later in the same function body (fresh LoRA path):

```python
if model_config_name:
    from model_training.d2l_probe import load_probe_cache
```

This is also correctly inside the function body. **No issue.**

### 3. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py` — `# type: ignore[assignment]` suppression pattern indicates a type-safety gap (mypy strict-ish)

The PR adds multiple `# type: ignore[assignment]` annotations when unpacking from `dict[str, object]`:

```python
resolved_rank: int = params["rank"]  # type: ignore[assignment]
resolved_alpha: int = params["alpha"]  # type: ignore[assignment]
resolved_epochs: int = params["epochs"]  # type: ignore[assignment]
resolved_grad_accum: int = params["grad_accum"]  # type: ignore[assignment]
resolved_lr_sched: str = params["lr_sched"]  # type: ignore[assignment]
model_id: str = resolved_model_id or os.environ.get(...)  # type: ignore[assignment]
```

The root cause is that `_resolve_training_params` returns `dict[str, object]` rather than a `TypedDict`. With a strict-ish mypy config, the proper fix is to use a `TypedDict` for the return type instead of suppressing the errors. Accumulating 5+ `type: ignore` suppressions in a few consecutive lines for the same underlying issue (untyped dict) is a real type-safety gap. CLAUDE.md requires "mypy with strict-ish config," and this pattern works around rather than satisfies mypy.

**Flag:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py`, lines added around the `_resolve_training_params` call site (lines ~140-160 in the new file). `_resolve_training_params` should return a `TypedDict` to avoid the 5 suppression comments.

### 4. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_train.py` — `model_post_init` docstring is not Google style

The added method:

```python
def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
    """Resolve base_model_name from registry when not explicitly set."""
```

This is a one-liner summary with no `Args:` section. Google style for a method that takes a non-self argument (`__context`) should document the argument. This is a minor gap — the `__context` is a Pydantic internal and could reasonably be omitted, so this is borderline. **Borderline, not flagging.**

### 5. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/model_configs.py` — `list_names` method missing docstring body / Args section for convention consistency

```python
def list_names(self) -> list[str]:
    """Return sorted list of registered model names."""
```

This is a one-liner with no `Args:` or `Returns:` section. Google style requires at minimum a `Returns:` section for any non-trivial return. This is a minor omission but technically violates Google docstring style.

**Flag:** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/model_configs.py`, `ModelRegistry.list_names()`. The one-line docstring has no `Returns:` section as required by Google style for functions with return values.

### 6. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py` — `train_and_register` has model_id fallback logic duplicated outside `_resolve_training_params`

```python
model_id = base_model_id
if model_id is None and model_config_name:
    from model_training.model_configs import ModelRegistry
    model_id = ModelRegistry.default().get(model_config_name).model_id
if model_id is None:
    model_id = os.environ.get("RUNE_BASE_MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct")
```

`train_qlora` already calls `_resolve_training_params` which handles this resolution. `train_and_register` then re-resolves it separately to pass `base_model_id` to the registry call. This is not a CLAUDE.md violation per se — it's a design smell (duplication). **Not flagging as a convention violation.**

### 7. `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py` — `rank` recorded in registry falls back to hardcoded `64` instead of resolved value

```python
rank=rank if rank is not None else 64,
```

This is called in `train_and_register` when recording the adapter to the registry. But `train_qlora` internally resolves rank from the model registry (e.g., 64 for qwen3.5-9b, 8 for qwen3-coder-next). If a non-default model config is used, the registry record will store `64` regardless of the actual rank used for training. This is a correctness bug, not strictly a CLAUDE.md convention issue. **Noting as a correctness concern, not a convention flag.**

---

## Summary of Real Convention Violations

| # | File | Approx. Location | Issue |
|---|------|-----------------|-------|
| 1 | `libs/model-training/src/model_training/trainer.py` | `_resolve_training_params` return type + caller site | Returns `dict[str, object]` forcing 5 consecutive `# type: ignore[assignment]` suppressions at the call site. Should use a `TypedDict` to satisfy mypy strict-ish without suppression. |
| 2 | `libs/model-training/src/model_training/model_configs.py` | `ModelRegistry.list_names()` | One-line docstring missing `Returns:` section; Google style requires it for methods with non-trivial return values. |

One additional correctness concern (not a convention violation) worth flagging to the author: `train_and_register` records `rank=rank if rank is not None else 64` to the adapter registry, but the actual training rank is resolved via `_resolve_training_params` from the model config (e.g., rank 8 for qwen3-coder-next). If training runs with `model_config_name="qwen3-coder-next"` and no explicit `rank`, the stored registry rank will be `64` while the adapter was actually trained at rank `8`.
